Problems & improvements

问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39335502311 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

重要指引错误上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · API 指引劝阻 TanStack Query，却没有给出跨页面缓存失效方案

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

停止诊断只允许单次只读调用；保留待定向复核的候选。

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    api.md 第 235 行说没有共享数据加载 hook，并让读者不要使用 Refine 数据 hooks 或 TanStack Query；但框架自己的站内通知 hook 就使用 QueryClient，并在 mutation 后 invalidateQueries。应用因此手写 useTicketsApi，只维护组件内 revision，verify-6 中创建/分派后列表保持旧数据。

    评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

    api.md：没有共享数据加载 hook，使用 useApiClient+useEffect；不要使用 Refine 的 useList/useTable/useForm 或 TanStack Query。

    评审时 · 实际观察

    notification-in-app 的 useInboxActions 使用 useQueryClient 并在 onSettled 调用 invalidateQueries；应用也自建 QueryClient。按指引手写的工单列表没有失效路径，verify-6 记录必须整页刷新。

    实际影响
    照指引实现会得到无共享失效的本地状态，产生静默陈旧列表；用户可能重复分派或误判工单状态。
    建议改法
    修正“不要用 TanStack Query”的一刀切表述，明确哪些场景由应用自管缓存；在 api.md 增加 mutation 成功后 invalidateQueries 的跨页面示例，或提供 app-client 级统一失效入口。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    创建或分派工单成功并返回列表
    应有能力 / 行为
    共享查询缓存被失效，列表显示最新记录
    实际观察
    useTicketsApi 只重取本地 revision，列表保持旧数据直到整页刷新
    应用绕行与代价
    用户整页刷新；应用可自行引入 QueryClient 并手动失效
    改进后的验收标准
    创建/分派成功后列表无需刷新出现新记录；文档示例能在两个页面间验证失效，通知 hook 与业务列表使用一致的缓存约定
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:5–8E84

    API 指引要求从 @nocobase/app-client 使用 useApiClient/apiClientToken/ApiClientError，禁止自建 client、fetch/axios 或硬编码 /api。

    - **Do not** create your own client, do not use `fetch` or axios, do not hard-code `/api`, and do not build endpoint URLs from `location`. The client's base URL (the configuration key `api.baseURL`, by default `resolveAppUrl('/api')`) already includes the deployment base path (for example `/main`).
- For a URL served by the application, such as an API download `href`, an API-backed image or a page link, use `resolveAppUrl('/api/…')` from `@nocobase/app-client`, which adds the runtime deployment base path. Keep runtime-generated resources on this helper.
- For a static file shipped in the frontend build, use `resolveAssetUrl('/assets/logo.png')` from `@nocobase/app-client`. Paths are relative to the client output root (`dist/client/assets/logo.png` in this example), with or without a leading slash. The helper uses Vite's absolute HTTP(S) `base` when configured, and otherwise falls back to `resolveAppUrl`; existing absolute, protocol-relative, data and blob URLs are preserved. The templates set `base` from `CDN_BASE_URL` during the build, and Vite writes it into the client code as `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`. Changing CDN settings requires rebuilding and uploading the matching files. Business code should call the helper rather than read the build variable itself.
- Import `useApiClient`, `apiClientToken`, the `ApiClient` type and `ApiClientError` from `@nocobase/app-client`. That way the application and plugins share one runtime and one error class, which is what makes `instanceof ApiClientError` reliable.
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:235–235E85

    API 指引称没有共享数据加载 hook，要求组件内 useApiClient+useEffect，并明确劝阻使用 Refine 数据 hooks 或 TanStack Query。

    There is no shared data-loading hook; write it directly in the component: `useApiClient()` + `useEffect` + `AbortController`. Do not reach for the data hooks of the installed Refine packages (`useList`, `useTable`, `useForm` from `@refinedev/*`) or for TanStack Query: the application registers no Refine data provider, so those hooks have nothing to call, and one loading pattern across pages keeps states and errors consistent. The application shell mounts Refine and `client/service-provider.ts` can register Refine resources, which only describe records to code that opts into Refine; without a data provider they load nothing, and they never add menu entries. Add a data provider only when the user asks to adopt Refine's data hooks across the application. The component below loads a project by id and displays it, covering loading, retry after failure, record not found, no permission and empty values (`client/pages/projects/project-summary.tsx`):
    packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/client/inbox/use-inbox.js:63–68E86

    站内通知的 useInboxActions 在 mutation 结束后 invalidateQueries(inboxKeys.all)，并先做乐观更新/失败回滚；这是框架内已实现的缓存一致性模式。

    /** Marks, deletes and reads all, updating the cached list and count at once and refetching both once settled. */
export function useInboxActions() {
    const client = useApiClient();
    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    const settle = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: inboxKeys.all });
    const mark = useMutation({
    app/client/pages/tickets/shared.ts:149–184E98

    应用自写 useTicketsApi 只用组件内 revision 重新请求；创建/分派成功后没有共享查询键或 invalidate，列表保持旧结果直到整页刷新。

    export function useTicketsApi<T>(path: string | null) {
  const api = useApiClient();
  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{
    path: string | null;
    revision: number;
    data?: T;
    error?: unknown;
    loading: boolean;
  }>({ path, revision: -1, loading: true });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!path) return;
    let active = true;
    void api.request<T>({ path }).then(
      (body) => {
        // Keep the response envelope: list endpoints carry `meta` beside
        // `data`, and every caller reads the ticket payload from `data.data`.
        if (active) setResult({ path, revision, data: body, loading: false });
      },
      (error: unknown) => {
        if (active) setResult({ path, revision, error, loading: false });
      },
    );
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [api, path, revision]);

  return {
    data: result.path === path ? result.data : undefined,
    error: result.path === path ? result.error : undefined,
    loading:
      path !== null && (result.path !== path || result.revision !== revision),
    reload: useCallback(() => setRevision((value) => value + 1), []),
  };

    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [重要][指引错误] API 指引劝阻 TanStack Query，却没有给出跨页面缓存失效方案

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md`、`@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.25）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

停止诊断只允许单次只读调用；保留待定向复核的候选。


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

api.md 第 235 行说没有共享数据加载 hook，并让读者不要使用 Refine 数据 hooks 或 TanStack Query；但框架自己的站内通知 hook 就使用 QueryClient，并在 mutation 后 invalidateQueries。应用因此手写 useTicketsApi，只维护组件内 revision，verify-6 中创建/分派后列表保持旧数据。

**文档 / API 声称**：api.md：没有共享数据加载 hook，使用 useApiClient+useEffect；不要使用 Refine 的 useList/useTable/useForm 或 TanStack Query。

**实际观察**：notification-in-app 的 useInboxActions 使用 useQueryClient 并在 onSettled 调用 invalidateQueries；应用也自建 QueryClient。按指引手写的工单列表没有失效路径，verify-6 记录必须整页刷新。

**影响**：照指引实现会得到无共享失效的本地状态，产生静默陈旧列表；用户可能重复分派或误判工单状态。

**建议改动**：修正“不要用 TanStack Query”的一刀切表述，明确哪些场景由应用自管缓存；在 api.md 增加 mutation 成功后 invalidateQueries 的跨页面示例，或提供 app-client 级统一失效入口。

**验收标准**：创建/分派成功后列表无需刷新出现新记录；文档示例能在两个页面间验证失效，通知 hook 与业务列表使用一致的缓存约定

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:5–8` — API 指引要求从 @nocobase/app-client 使用 useApiClient/apiClientToken/ApiClientError，禁止自建 client、fetch/axios 或硬编码 /api。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:235–235` — API 指引称没有共享数据加载 hook，要求组件内 useApiClient+useEffect，并明确劝阻使用 Refine 数据 hooks 或 TanStack Query。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/client/inbox/use-inbox.js:63–68` — 站内通知的 useInboxActions 在 mutation 结束后 invalidateQueries(inboxKeys.all)，并先做乐观更新/失败回滚；这是框架内已实现的缓存一致性模式。
- `app/client/pages/tickets/shared.ts:149–184` — 应用自写 useTicketsApi 只用组件内 revision 重新请求；创建/分派成功后没有共享查询键或 invalidate，列表保持旧结果直到整页刷新。
- `artifacts/agent-history/verify-6/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0009.txt:2209–2211` — verify-6/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl 第 28543-28545 行原始事件：Agent Browser acceptance failed: { "passed": false, "authenticated": true, "summary": "报修提交（含截图）、服务台分派、工程师处理与解决、员工退回后再次处理、员工确认关闭、通知跳转、角色权限隔离和首页待分派/超时/工作量均已实际走通。但工单列表在新建或分派后不刷新，必须整页刷新才显示最新数据，已记录为失败。",

    F8 · 认证 Skill 未记录 preparation/retryPreparation，Agent 无法发现实时失败后的官方回退

    反馈证据核对

    停止诊断只允许单次只读调用；保留待定向复核的候选。

      反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

      认证实现暴露 preparing/ready/failed、retryPreparation 与 refresh，但 SKILL.md 第 48 行把 useAuthentication() 只列为 { client, session, isPending, refresh }。Agent 读到的是“会话已就绪与否”，没有契约说明实时准备失败后仍应继续 HTTP 会话，最终写出阻塞式页面。

      实际影响
      实时连接抖动会升级为全应用不可用；应用开发者/Agent 只能从 dist 类型或源码反推回退方式。
      建议改法
      在认证 Skill 的 useAuthentication 契约中补齐 preparation、retryPreparation、refresh 与失败后的推荐回退，并链接 client-session-and-pages；明确实时准备不是登录门槛。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      已登录但实时 socket 未建立，Agent 按认证 Skill 实现 session gate
      应有能力 / 行为
      Skill 说明 preparation 失败可重试并继续 HTTP，应用有界等待
      实际观察
      Skill 只列 session/isPending/refresh，应用把 preparation 失败渲染成阻塞页
      应用绕行与代价
      应用自行加 3 秒 grace 与 watchdog；本报告从 dist 实现反推契约
      改进后的验收标准
      按 Skill 示例实现时，阻断 WS 仍进入应用；Skill 的契约字段与 dist 类型一致，并有一条失败回退验收
      评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:48–51E81

      认证 Skill 把 useAuthentication() 描述为 { client, session, isPending, refresh }，没有列出实现已暴露的 preparation/retryPreparation；读者无法从指引得知实时准备失败后的官方回退入口。

      - `useAuthentication()` returns `{ client, session, isPending, refresh }`;
  `useAuthenticationClient()` returns the Better Auth client alone.
- `RequiredAuthentication`, `GuestAuthentication`, and
  `AuthenticationGuard({ mode })` gate a route subtree on the session.
      packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:99–112E80

      useAuthentication 对外暴露 preparation（ready/preparing/failed）、retryPreparation 和 refresh，页面可以据此决定等待、重试或继续 HTTP 会话。

          const preparation = !signedIn
        ? 'ready'

      app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:18–39E95

      最终应用把实时准备等待限制为 3 秒，超时后记录告警、失效授权快照并继续 HTTP 会话，不再渲染阻塞式 Unable to connect。

      export const SESSION_PREPARATION_GRACE_MS = 3_000;

/** The first identity handshake invalidates permissions before pages mount. */
export function SessionPreparation({
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement {
  const { preparation, session } = useAuthentication();
  const authorization = useAuthorizationClient();
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const sessionKey = session ? `${session.user.id}:${session.session.id}` : '';
  const [grace, setGrace] = useState({ sessionKey: '', elapsed: false });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preparation !== 'preparing') return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      console.warn(
        'Realtime session preparation did not finish in time; continuing with HTTP authorization.',
      );
      // Entering without the realtime identity handshake still must not reuse the previous session's snapshot.
      authorization.invalidate();
      setGrace({ sessionKey, elapsed: true });
    }, SESSION_PREPARATION_GRACE_MS);

      ### [重要][指引缺失] 认证 Skill 未记录 preparation/retryPreparation，Agent 无法发现实时失败后的官方回退

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.10）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

停止诊断只允许单次只读调用；保留待定向复核的候选。


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

认证实现暴露 preparing/ready/failed、retryPreparation 与 refresh，但 SKILL.md 第 48 行把 useAuthentication() 只列为 { client, session, isPending, refresh }。Agent 读到的是“会话已就绪与否”，没有契约说明实时准备失败后仍应继续 HTTP 会话，最终写出阻塞式页面。

**影响**：实时连接抖动会升级为全应用不可用；应用开发者/Agent 只能从 dist 类型或源码反推回退方式。

**建议改动**：在认证 Skill 的 useAuthentication 契约中补齐 preparation、retryPreparation、refresh 与失败后的推荐回退，并链接 client-session-and-pages；明确实时准备不是登录门槛。

**验收标准**：按 Skill 示例实现时，阻断 WS 仍进入应用；Skill 的契约字段与 dist 类型一致，并有一条失败回退验收

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:48–51` — 认证 Skill 把 useAuthentication() 描述为 { client, session, isPending, refresh }，没有列出实现已暴露的 preparation/retryPreparation；读者无法从指引得知实时准备失败后的官方回退入口。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:99–112` — useAuthentication 对外暴露 preparation（ready/preparing/failed）、retryPreparation 和 refresh，页面可以据此决定等待、重试或继续 HTTP 会话。
- `artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0001.txt:1157–1157` — verify-1 主页面正文显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared. Retry to enter the application, or sign out.”，登录后 shell 被实时准备失败挡住。
- `artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0002.txt:203–205` — 同一轮手工执行 new WebSocket('ws://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ws') 返回 “open”，证明服务端 WS 可用，而应用未建立连接。
- `app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:18–39` — 最终应用把实时准备等待限制为 3 秒，超时后记录告警、失效授权快照并继续 HTTP 会话，不再渲染阻塞式 Unable to connect。

        packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/task-container.js:1–8E91

        独立数据库任务的 container 是冻结空容器，除显式提供的 token 外 resolve 会抛错，避免迁移/种子误用应用级数据库管理器。

        /** Standalone database tasks have no application services unless supplied by their owner. */
export const emptyDatabaseTaskContainer = Object.freeze({
    has: () => false,
    resolve(token) {
        throw new Error(`Service "${token.name}" is not registered in the database task container.`);
    },
    resolveIfCreated: () => undefined,
});
        packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:188–199E92

        nocobase-db Skill 规定迁移默认用 builder/query，repository 仅用于 query 难以正确表达的写入，并提醒 repository 是在押注迁移自身未声明的 Collection 形状。

        | Context   | Tools                            | Default for data |
| --------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------- |
| Migration | `builder`, `query`, `repository` | `query`          |
| Seed      | `repository`, `query`            | `repository`     |

A migration changes structure, so `builder` and `query` are its tools and `repository` is reserved for the writes `query` would get wrong: cross-dialect field encoding and decoding, Collection-level naming overrides, and relation writes including the junction rows behind a `belongsToMany`. **Anything `query` expresses correctly stays on `query`.** A seed changes no structure and writes installation data in Collection terms, so `repository` is its normal tool and `query` covers what that cannot express, such as reading a physical table backing no Collection.

This does not loosen the self-contained rule. `repository(name)` resolves the Collection from the database itself — the metadata a previous `builder` operation wrote — which is why it is available at all; importing or iterating the application's own collection definitions from a migration stays forbidden. But a migration using it is betting on a shape it did not declare in its own body, so three constraints follow:

- Say in a comment why `query` was not enough. Nothing static can check this, and the comment is what separates a considered use from a reflex.
- Do not use it in `down`. By the time a rollback runs, the Collection has moved past what `up` left behind, possibly several migrations past.
- Do not use it to walk a table. Selecting every row and updating each one through a Repository is the shape to avoid; a set-based `query` statement is both correct and bounded.

        Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md

        ⌄

        未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

        verify-1 中登录和 get-session 成功，但应用没有构造 /main/ws，页面停留在“Unable to connect”。认证 Provider 在 10 秒后发布 failed，并暴露 retryPreparation，但认证 Skill 只描述 session/isPending/refresh，没有给出准备失败后的回退契约；初始应用因此把 failed 渲染成阻塞页。

        连续多轮验收都失败但失败指纹和根因不同
        应有能力 / 行为
        停止报告区分重复失败与逐轮新缺陷，并给出每轮修复推进情况
        实际观察
        诊断只显示修复次数已达上限，需人工从多份日志还原不同根因
        应用绕行与代价
        本报告逐轮对照原始事件与源码进行归因
        改进后的验收标准
        下一次停止报告直接列出指纹分组、每轮新增/残留问题及是否建议继续修复
        E94 E71 E77 E78 E99
        实现者过程与运行背景 · 10 个过程问题 / 13 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

        本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

        本轮过程记录

        Fixed the helpdesk sign-in lockout by making realtime readiness a bounded wait instead of a requirement for authenticated HTTP routes, and by supervising and guarding the realtime connection so a failed socket attempt cannot unmount the application. Also made format:check ignore the locally generated config.yml, which the factory excludes from its own auto-formatting but not from the application's Prettier ignore file. This round repaired the ticket child routes, made the engineer queue assignment-only, restored requester close/reopen, and installed the demo from a sample seed during the factory's separate test installation. A fresh test apply now contains the five demo accounts, their jobs, and eight demo tickets in every desk state; a production apply contains only the administrator and no tickets. A production build/API pass also caught that reopen with a reason failed because the lifecycle declared an accepted field the ticket table did not have; that was fixed and re-verified through the real endpoint. This round fixed the app-server verification failure: the requester close/reopen grant migration now skips when the authorization plugin's permission-set Collections are not registered, instead of failing the whole migration run.

        问题 01 · verification

        Sign-in succeeded but every authenticated route stayed behind realtime preparation

        本任务已处理

        After a successful username sign-in and a valid get-session response, the page stayed on 'Preparing your session…' and then showed 'Unable to connect' with Retry and Sign out. Retry, reload, and reopening the session repeated it; no new /main/ws WebSocket was constructed, while a manually opened socket succeeded.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The signed-in shell treated an opened realtime socket as a prerequisite for mounting any authenticated route. The realtime client has no recovery when a reconnect attempt does not reach `new WebSocket` (an early return or a constructor failure), so the authentication provider's preparation stayed preparing until its ten-second timeout and then blocked the whole application behind the failure screen. Realtime is optional for authorization, which is fetched over HTTP and invalidated on session changes.
        处理方式
        Bound session preparation to three seconds in client/routing/session-preparation.tsx; on expiry it invalidates the authorization snapshot and mounts the application while the realtime client keeps trying. Added client/lib/realtime-watchdog.ts: DefaultClientServiceProvider guards `reconnect()` so a throwing constructor cannot escape the authentication effect, and a watchdog forces a fresh connection every five seconds while subscriptions are active, the socket is closed, the tab is visible, and the browser is online. Updated the shell rule and the session-preparation tests to describe and verify the HTTP fallback.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 02 · diagnosis

        The verifier's exact socket-construction failure could not be reproduced locally

        本任务已处理

        Normal sign-in and reload against the same production server and database opened /main/ws and reached the business UI; the original failing browser state was not available for inspection.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The failure depended on an environment or browser state in which the realtime client's reconnect path returned without constructing a WebSocket or threw during construction. The application code had no diagnostic or recovery path for that state, so the exact trigger could not be observed from source alone.
        处理方式
        Covered both observable failure shapes with deterministic tests: a never-opening WebSocket and a throwing WebSocket constructor. Verified both in the production build at http://127.0.0.1:13000/main, including that the throwing constructor no longer blanks the page and that restoring a working constructor lets the watchdog open /main/ws.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 03 · verification

        format:check failed on the locally generated config.yml

        本任务已处理

        `pnpm format:check` exited 1 with `[warn] config.yml` and `Code style issues found in the above file`.

        原因（实现者自述）
        `pnpm nocobase config init` writes config.yml with a generated secrets key in double quotes, and deployment writes its own copy. The file is local configuration the factory owns and explicitly excludes from its `format:auto` stage, and `.gitignore` un-ignores it, so Prettier 3 checked it even though the application must not reformat or edit it.
        处理方式
        Added `config.yml` to `.prettierignore` with a comment explaining that it is generated local configuration and that the factory already skips it when auto-formatting. The file itself was left untouched. `pnpm format:check '!.github/**'` now passes.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 04 · verification

        Ticket create and detail pages never appeared under the list route

        本任务已处理

        Clicking a ticket or the create action changed the URL but the list stayed visible; the new-ticket form and ticket detail could not be reached, and the QA pass reported that employees could not submit tickets or service desk could not process them.

        原因（实现者自述）
        client/pages/tickets/index.tsx was declared as the parent route but rendered no <Outlet />, so react-router had nowhere to mount /tickets/new and /tickets/:ticketId. The child pages also used a bare PageContainer instead of RouteChildPage, which would have stacked them below the parent rather than covering it.
        处理方式
        Added <Outlet /> to the parent ticket list container and wrapped every create/detail return path in RouteChildPage. Added tests/components/tickets-routes.test.tsx and browser-verified the covering form/detail layers for Alice, Bob, and Dana.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 05 · verification

        Engineers could read unassigned pending tickets outside their queue

        本任务已处理

        The support-engineer ticket scope exposed unassigned pending tickets in addition to tickets assigned to the engineer, so the queue and dashboard did not match the stated visibility rule.

        原因（实现者自述）
        helpdesk.engineerTickets in server/tickets/resources.ts combined an assignee condition with anyScope/allScopes, allowing unassigned pending rows through the same repository policy used by the list and dashboard.
        处理方式
        Changed the scope to a single assigneeId = current principal condition and removed the unassigned/all-scope branches. The engineer queue copy now says it shows only tickets assigned to me. Existing lifecycle tests and the Bob API/browser checks confirm only assigned tickets are visible.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 06 · verification

        Requesters could not close a resolved ticket or reopen a closed one

        本任务已处理

        Employees could read their own tickets but lacked the close and reopen actions required by the confirmation flow; service desk also lacked close by design, leaving a resolved ticket without a requester confirmation path.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The employee permission seed and lifecycle guards only granted the employee read/create/comment/attach actions. Reopen was guarded by a service-desk role and close by an administrator role, neither of which matched the requester-owned confirmation flow.
        处理方式
        Added close and reopen to the employee permission set with the own-ticket scope; introduced requesterClose and requesterReopen lifecycle guards; updated the client/server locale keys; added migration 202610200001_grant_requester_close_reopen for existing installs; and added lifecycle tests for requester success and non-requester rejection.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 07 · verification

        A fresh factory test install had no sample accounts or tickets

        本任务已处理

        The QA browser saw only the Super Admin and an empty Tickets list even though the task requested sample data. A fresh NODE_ENV=test db apply created one user and zero tickets.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The factory installs the database with NODE_ENV=test, then starts the application with NODE_ENV=production. The helpdesk demo was registered only on sampleDataToken, which runs during application start; it never ran in the pre-apply process, and the later production start treated the database as already installed. A seed cannot resolve application services because the database task container deliberately exposes only repository/query access.
        处理方式
        Added sample seed database/main/seeds/202610200002_helpdesk_demo_data.ts, marked sample: true and guarded against production. It writes stable-id demo users, credential accounts, job assignments, and eight tickets in pending/processing/escalated/resolved/closed/cancelled states directly through the seed repository. The sampleDataToken provider still enriches the same ticket titles through the lifecycle on a direct development start. Added tests/logic/helpdesk-sample-seed.test.ts for idempotency, password verification, and the production guard. Verified a fresh NODE_ENV=test apply contains 5 demo users, 8 assignments, and 8 tickets, while a NODE_ENV=production apply contains only the administrator and no tickets.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 08 · verification

        Executed permission seed no longer matches its edited source checksum

        处理结果未提供

        db apply on the existing development database prints: Executed seed "202610070001_helpdesk_permission_sets" no longer matches its source.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The employee grant was corrected in the seed source after that seed had already been executed on storage/database.sqlite. NocoBase records the source checksum and requires an explicit repair to accept the edit.
        处理方式
        Kept the corrected immutable-style seed source and documented the warning. Did not run db repair --force because the task forbids bypassing the confirmation; fresh installs, including the factory verification database, execute the corrected seed without a warning.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 09 · verification

        Reopen failed through the real API when it carried a reason

        本任务已处理

        The lifecycle unit test accepted reopen with a reason, but POST /api/tickets/:id/fire with transition reopen returned 400 FAILED_PRECONDITION: Field "reason" does not exist. The closed ticket stayed closed.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The reopen transition declared accept: ["reason"]. @nocobase/lifecycle copies accepted input into the record update, and the tickets table has no reason column. The in-memory lifecycle test kit did not persist the accepted field, so it could not expose the mismatch.
        处理方式
        Removed reason from the transition accept list. The reason remains in the transition input and audit history, which is where the lifecycle stores it. Added a definition assertion to tests/logic/helpdesk.test.ts, rebuilt the production server, and verified Bob receives 404 for reopen while Alice receives 200 and the ticket returns to pending with its assignee and close timestamps cleared.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 10 · verification

        Database migrations failed on an embedded app that registered no plugins

        本任务已处理

        tests/logic/app-server.test.ts > registers application-owned providers and routes from the runtime failed with AppDatabaseTaskError: Database migrations failed for connection "main": Collection "authorizationPermissionSets" does not exist, raised from 202610200001_grant_requester_close_reopen.

        原因（实现者自述）
        The migration unconditionally resolved authorizationPermissionSets through context.repository, but that Collection belongs to the authorization plugin. The failing test boots an embedded scope with defineServerPlugins([]) plus one test plugin, so the plugin never registers its Collections and the repository guard threw COLLECTION_NOT_FOUND before the test could check its providers and route.
        处理方式
        Guarded both up and down with context.builder.hasCollection('authorizationPermissionSets') and returned without writing when the Collection is absent; the builder lookup resolves installed metadata records, so a real installation still receives the grants even when plugins are not loaded during a CLI apply. Added tests/logic/helpdesk-grant-migration.test.ts covering the absent-Collection skip, the append of reopen/close while preserving other grants, the rollback removal, and the already-granted no-op. Re-ran app-server.test.ts, helpdesk.test.ts, the new test, the server typecheck, and ESLint/Prettier on the changed files.
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

        建议 05 · 验收机制

        Keep generated local configuration out of the Prettier check

        可自动化

        The factory's format:auto deliberately skips config.yml because it is generated and factory-owned, but the subsequent format:check had no matching ignore, so a generated file could fail a task independently of application code.

        建议改动

        Add config.yml to .prettierignore whenever an application keeps a generated config.yml in its workspace.

        来源：Agent · retro.json

        建议 11 · 验收机制

        Verify sample data on the factory fresh-install path, not only on the existing database

        可自动化

        The existing development database already had samples from an earlier application start, which hid the fact that a fresh db apply produced only the administrator. The QA acceptance report exposed the gap.

        建议改动

        Run a temp-config NODE_ENV=test db apply and assert the expected sample users/tickets before considering sample data delivered.

      "resolution": "Guarded both up and down with context.builder.hasCollection('authorizationPermissionSets') and returned without writing when the Collection is absent; the builder lookup resolves installed metadata records, so a real installation still receives the grants even when plugins are not loaded during a CLI apply. Added tests/logic/helpdesk-grant-migration.test.ts covering the absent-Collection skip, the append of reopen/close while preserving other grants, the rollback removal, and the already-granted no-op. Re-ran app-server.test.ts, helpdesk.test.ts, the new test, the server typecheck, and ESLint/Prettier on the changed files.",
      "status": "resolved"
    }
  ],
  "improvements": [
    "Do not make optional realtime connectivity a gate for authenticated HTTP pages; bound the wait and keep retrying in the background.",
    "Supervise long-lived client connections from the application service provider so a wedged socket cannot require a full reload.",
    "Keep reconnect failures inside the realtime layer; an exception from an authentication effect can otherwise unmount the entire signed-in tree.",
    "Test both a never-opening socket and a throwing WebSocket constructor, not only a mocked successful onOpen.",
    {
      "category": "verification",

      "mechanizable": true
    },
    "A parent page route that owns child paths must render <Outlet />; child pages should use RouteChildPage so they cover the parent instead of stacking below it.",
    "Keep a role scope as narrow as the stated visibility rule; do not include unassigned rows in an engineer queue merely to make claiming convenient.",
    "When an ownership flow spans roles, name the guards after the owning relationship (requesterClose/requesterReopen) rather than the role that happened to hold the action.",
    "A fresh-install sample must be installed by a sample seed when installation and application start are separate processes; sampleDataToken alone is too late.",

        迁移器能按历史/checksum 执行并在失败时停止，任务容器也刻意隔离应用服务；缺口在指引没有提醒嵌入式测试的最小插件集，导致应用迁移把插件集合当必然存在。

        E91 E93

        使用便利度

        70/100

        迁移 API 与错误信息清楚（COLLECTION_NOT_FOUND 直接指出集合），但修复需要应用自己加存在性判断；框架没有提供“可选集合”助手。

        E91 E93

        Agent 友好度

        64/100

        Skill 已警告 repository 是在押注未声明的 Collection 形状，却没说明测试 app 可能不安装插件集合，Agent 在 verify-5 才通过失败堆栈发现。

        E92 E99

        设计合理性

        80/100

        迁移上下文用冻结 task container，迁移器集中处理历史、锁、checksum 与 pending 选择，边界和职责清楚。

        E91 E93

        实现完整性与可靠性

        78/100

        迁移器按批执行、校验历史并停止于失败；任务容器阻止误用应用级 manager。实际失败是应用前置条件缺失，最终用 hasCollection 守卫解决。

        child-routes.md 明确父页面自己放 Outlet；通知 hook 用统一 query key 在 mutation settle 后失效；迁移任务容器使用冻结容器并配合 Skill 的 repository 警告。三处都把容易踩的边界写成了可检查的规则或现成实现。

        提供的帮助
        Agent 可以直接复用这些边界，减少自行发明路由/缓存/迁移约定的风险；本次失败主要是应用没有遵循或没有落实到页面。

When adopting the files into the regular suite, use the application's existing environment selection or Vitest projects and keep `tests/playwright/**` excluded from Vitest. Preserve existing aliases, setup files and test discovery.\n\n### Fixture selection and ownership\n\n| What the test exercises                                   | Helper                                                       | Isolation and cleanup                                                                                                |\n| --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Repositories and transactions without application startup | `createDatabaseTest()` from `/server`                        | `isolation: 'schema'` resets and migrates before each case; the fixture destroys connections and drops its databases |\n| A migration's schema and rollback                         | `describeMigration()` from `/server`                         | Owns its database and checks apply → rollback → reapply; `up` assertions run twice                                   |\n| Routes, providers, plugins and authentication             | `createAppTest()` from `/server`                             | Default `scope: 'file'` shares an application; `scope: 'test'` installs a fresh one per case; cleanup is automatic   |\n| A manually started test application                       | `createTestApp()` from `/server`                             | The caller closes it in `finally` with `await testApp.close()`                                                       |\n| Components using application services                     | `renderWithApp()` from `/client`                             | Call inside a test; client shutdown is automatic and the React preset cleans up the DOM                              |\n| A command operating on files or flags                     | `bindAppCommand()` + `runAppCommand()` from `/cli`           | The test owns its temporary files; command output capture is process-wide                                            |\n| A command opening the real application                    | `createTestAppConfig()` + `bindTestAppCommand()` from `/cli` | The caller disposes the configuration in `finally`; `withApp()` closes the command's application                     |\n\nFor database, server and database-backed CLI tests, `NOCOBASE_TEST_DB_DIALECT` selects the dialect; unset means SQLite. Provision every application-owned connection by listing it in `connections`. An omitted connection keeps the application's configuration. Keep provisioned connection locations intact; use test configuration overrides for auth, seeds or other settings. Pure repository tests take explicit migration and seed sources; application fixtures install the application's registered sources by default.\n\nRun database and server suites sequentially. CLI stream capture and environment stubs are process-wide, so do not use concurrent command cases. Keep temporary storage under the fixture's ownership, and never clean up a shared development database or a running Vite cache.\n\n### Run a complete example\n\nAfter creating the files in the linked examples, run from the application root:\n\n```bash\npnpm exec vitest run --config vitest.database.config.ts\npnpm exec vitest run --config vitest.server.config.ts\npnpm exec vitest run --config vitest.client.config.ts\npnpm exec vitest run --config vitest.cli.config.ts\n```\n\nRun these commands one at a time. With all four groups copied, expect five database tests (including the migration), five server tests, five client tests and eight CLI tests (six file-command cases and two database-backed cases). A copied subset has the corresponding smaller count. The sample configurations set `passWithNoTests: false`; when using an existing configuration, still confirm that the named files ran.\n\nIf startup fails, check the installed package exports, the selected driver, the fixture's migration sources and the application's registered plugins before replacing a real service with a mock. A missing collection in a repository test usually needs its migration sources; failed sign-in needs the configured test seed and trusted origin; a server `403` with a valid session needs the intended grants. Client translation failures need the real namespace and locale keys, and CLI JSON failures need assertions on `exitCode` and the JSON error envelope.\n\n## Where tests go\n\n```text\ntests/logic/        Logic and integration tests\ntests/components/   Component tests\ntests/playwright/   Browser tests against a running application at APP_URL (Playwright; Vitest skips it)\n```\n\n**Never put a test beside the source it covers.** Name files `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx`.\n\n`vitest.config.ts` discovers `tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` automatically. Theme token tests compile the real CSS; browser checks still need to verify computed styles, typography, spacing and focus.\n\nTemplates ship their tests into generated applications. Keep them runnable from the application root after scaffolding: read application identity from `package.json`, resolve application paths relative to the test file, and import dependencies through their published package exports. Do not depend on a monorepo checkout, a fixed template directory name, or a particular pnpm store layout. Run the affected test files in the generated application as well as in the template when changing these contracts.\n\n## What to test, by change\n\n| You changed           | Test at least                                                                                                                           |\n| --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| A server route        | Anonymous → `401`, authenticated but unpermitted → `403`, permitted → expected payload; the route is declared in the API document       |\n| A list or stats route | Its query count does not grow with the number of rows or groups: count the reads for a few rows and for many, and assert they are equal |\n| A public webhook      | Missing signature, invalid signature, valid signature, duplicate delivery                                                               |\n| A migration           | `up` produces the expected schema; `down` reverses it; against a real database                                                          |\n| A seed                | First run, run against existing data, repeat run

After the n-th refused try a\n * continuation waits `backoffMs × factor^(n-1)`, capped at `maxMs`, before\n * the sweep tries it again, and each sweep tries at most `batchSize` of the\n * due ones, those due longest first. A `CONFLICT` is not counted and is\n * tried again after `backoffMs`. An error that is not a refusal, such as a\n * database failing mid-try, is not counted either, but backs off as a\n * refusal does, each such try taking the delay one step further (see\n * {@link PendingContinuation.errorTries}). After `maxAttempts` counted tries\n * the sweep gives up on it: see {@link PendingContinuation.abandonedAt}. So\n * only refusals lead to giving up: an outage never does, and neither does a\n * bug that throws an ordinary error, which is tried at most hourly and\n * logged on its 1st, 2nd, 4th… try until it is fixed.\n */\nexport interface ContinuationSweepOptions {\n    /** Defaults to 100. */\n    readonly batchSize?: number;\n    /** Defaults to a minute. */\n    readonly backoffMs?: number;\n    /** Defaults to 2. */\n    readonly factor?: number;\n    /** Defaults to an hour. */\n    readonly maxMs?: number;\n    /** Defaults to 10, which the default backoff reaches after about four hours. */\n    readonly maxAttempts?: number;\n}\n/**\n * Services, or a factory for them. Replace `main` with the target connection when needed:\n\n```ts\nimport { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';\n\nconst migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({\n  name: '202609080001_create_invoice_files',\n  async up({ builder }) {\n    await builder.createCollection('invoice_files', (collection) => {\n      collection.uuid('id').primary().notNull();\n      collection.string('disk', { length: 255 }).notNull();\n      collection.text('key').notNull();\n      collection.text('filename').notNull();\n      collection.string('ext', { length: 32 }).notNull();\n      collection.string('mimeType', { length: 255 }).notNull();\n      collection.bigInt('size').notNull();\n      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();\n      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();\n    });\n  },\n  async down({ builder }) {\n    await builder.dropCollection('invoice_files');\n  },\n});\nexport default migration;\n```\n\nThe fields are fixed; mapping is unsupported. Disk is required. Content paths contain static alphanumeric, underscore or hyphen segments, start with `/`, and have no trailing slash.\n\nOnly declared POST `/api/<name>/<action>` operations are exposed; `name` is a camelCase path segment such as `invoiceAttachments`. Ordinary actions are `findMany/findOne/count/exists/aggregate/groupBy/createOne/updateOne/deleteOne`. There are no createMany/updateMany/deleteMany HTTP actions.\n\nEvery exposed action appears in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`), under the exposure's name as its tag with operationIds such as `invoiceAttachmentsFindMany` and `invoiceAttachmentsUploadOne`. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.\n\nAn upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.\n\nContent is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.\n\nThese are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication.         E83 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/child-routes.md:9–15

        child-routes 指引写明子路由放入父路由 children，且父页面必须自己放置 <Outlet />；RouteChildPage、RouteDialog、RouteDrawer 都不会自动插入。

        - **Child routes go in the parent route's `children`**, declared in `client/routes.ts`, not in page component files.
- A child route's `path` is relative to its parent and is appended to the parent's path. A leading `/` is stripped before joining, so `new` and `/new` behave the same; this handbook writes the form without `/`.
- **The parent page must place `<Outlet />` itself**, where the child content should appear. Pages do not insert an Outlet automatically, and neither do `RouteChildPage`, `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`; only a plain navigation group gets its Outlet from the route renderer.
- **A child route that is a page of its own returns `RouteChildPage`.** Only tab content renders inline. A child route with a `PageContainer` of its own — a record's page, a form too long for a dialog, an import page — wraps it in `RouteChildPage` ([section 5](#5-covering-child-pages-routechildpage)). Returned bare, it renders at the parent's `Outlet`, below everything the parent shows, and the user has to scroll past the parent page to find it. A page that returns a bare `PageContainer` belongs on a top-level route instead.
- Child routes inherit the entry route's `auth` and cannot change it, and follow the `authz` inheritance rules in [section 4 of `page.md`](page.md#4-authz-page-authorization): a child page renders only after the parent page's check passes.
- Link to a child route with a relative path and keep the query string ([section 2.2 of `overlay.md`](overlay.md#22-place-the-outlet-in-the-parent-page)). A relative path resolves against the route that renders the link, not the URL on screen ([section 2 of `page.md`](page.md#2-the-page-component)).
- Route declarations have no `index` field. Do not invent an index route, and do not register a child route at the parent's own path; when "opening the parent URL shows a particular child page" is needed, use the redirect in [section 4](#4-page-tabs).

        原文件 SHA-256：53387eafc9638904295cd2d711f364fa0f3db48c0963c74ab45fc23199a800da

        E84 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:5–8

        API 指引要求从 @nocobase/app-client 使用 useApiClient/apiClientToken/ApiClientError，禁止自建 client、fetch/axios 或硬编码 /api。

        - **Do not** create your own client, do not use `fetch` or axios, do not hard-code `/api`, and do not build endpoint URLs from `location`. The client's base URL (the configuration key `api.baseURL`, by default `resolveAppUrl('/api')`) already includes the deployment base path (for example `/main`).
- For a URL served by the application, such as an API download `href`, an API-backed image or a page link, use `resolveAppUrl('/api/…')` from `@nocobase/app-client`, which adds the runtime deployment base path. Keep runtime-generated resources on this helper.
- For a static file shipped in the frontend build, use `resolveAssetUrl('/assets/logo.png')` from `@nocobase/app-client`. Paths are relative to the client output root (`dist/client/assets/logo.png` in this example), with or without a leading slash. The helper uses Vite's absolute HTTP(S) `base` when configured, and otherwise falls back to `resolveAppUrl`; existing absolute, protocol-relative, data and blob URLs are preserved. The templates set `base` from `CDN_BASE_URL` during the build, and Vite writes it into the client code as `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`. Changing CDN settings requires rebuilding and uploading the matching files. Business code should call the helper rather than read the build variable itself.
- Import `useApiClient`, `apiClientToken`, the `ApiClient` type and `ApiClientError` from `@nocobase/app-client`. That way the application and plugins share one runtime and one error class, which is what makes `instanceof ApiClientError` reliable.

        E85 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:235–235

        API 指引称没有共享数据加载 hook，要求组件内 useApiClient+useEffect，并明确劝阻使用 Refine 数据 hooks 或 TanStack Query。

        There is no shared data-loading hook; write it directly in the component: `useApiClient()` + `useEffect` + `AbortController`. Do not reach for the data hooks of the installed Refine packages (`useList`, `useTable`, `useForm` from `@refinedev/*`) or for TanStack Query: the application registers no Refine data provider, so those hooks have nothing to call, and one loading pattern across pages keeps states and errors consistent. The application shell mounts Refine and `client/service-provider.ts` can register Refine resources, which only describe records to code that opts into Refine; without a data provider they load nothing, and they never add menu entries. Add a data provider only when the user asks to adopt Refine's data hooks across the application. The component below loads a project by id and displays it, covering loading, retry after failure, record not found, no permission and empty values (`client/pages/projects/project-summary.tsx`):

        E89 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/record-access.js:37–51

        /** Builds a definition; `authz.recordAccess.define` registers it. */
export function defineRecordAccess(key, configure) {
    if (!key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    return configure(new RecordAccessBuilder({ key, collections: [] }));
}
function validateDefinition(definition) {
    if (!definition.key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    if (typeof definition.resolve !== 'function')
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires a resolver');
    if (!definition.collections.length ||
        definition.collections.some((name) => typeof name !== 'string' || !name))
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires collections');
    return definition;

        E92 · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:188–199

        nocobase-db Skill 规定迁移默认用 builder/query，repository 仅用于 query 难以正确表达的写入，并提醒 repository 是在押注迁移自身未声明的 Collection 形状。

        | Context   | Tools                            | Default for data |
| --------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------- |
| Migration | `builder`, `query`, `repository` | `query`          |
| Seed      | `repository`, `query`            | `repository`     |

A migration changes structure, so `builder` and `query` are its tools and `repository` is reserved for the writes `query` would get wrong: cross-dialect field encoding and decoding, Collection-level naming overrides, and relation writes including the junction rows behind a `belongsToMany`. **Anything `query` expresses correctly stays on `query`.** A seed changes no structure and writes installation data in Collection terms, so `repository` is its normal tool and `query` covers what that cannot express, such as reading a physical table backing no Collection.

This does not loosen the self-contained rule. `repository(name)` resolves the Collection from the database itself — the metadata a previous `builder` operation wrote — which is why it is available at all; importing or iterating the application's own collection definitions from a migration stays forbidden. But a migration using it is betting on a shape it did not declare in its own body, so three constraints follow:

- Say in a comment why `query` was not enough. Nothing static can check this, and the comment is what separates a considered use from a reflex.
- Do not use it in `down`. By the time a rollback runs, the Collection has moved past what `up` left behind, possibly several migrations past.
- Do not use it to walk a table. Selecting every row and updating each one through a Repository is the shape to avoid; a set-based `query` statement is both correct and bounded.

        原文件 SHA-256：b171ae2cf228b20abe83ea9e435dc2f7e333acf95065cba9cb70bc5bf6fd9d6b

        E95 · app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:18–39

        最终应用把实时准备等待限制为 3 秒，超时后记录告警、失效授权快照并继续 HTTP 会话，不再渲染阻塞式 Unable to connect。

        export const SESSION_PREPARATION_GRACE_MS = 3_000;

/** The first identity handshake invalidates permissions before pages mount. */
export function SessionPreparation({
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement {
  const { preparation, session } = useAuthentication();
  const authorization = useAuthorizationClient();
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const sessionKey = session ? `${session.user.id}:${session.session.id}` : '';
  const [grace, setGrace] = useState({ sessionKey: '', elapsed: false });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preparation !== 'preparing') return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      console.warn(
        'Realtime session preparation did not finish in time; continuing with HTTP authorization.',
      );
      // Entering without the realtime identity handshake still must not reuse the previous session's snapshot.
      authorization.invalidate();
      setGrace({ sessionKey, elapsed: true });
    }, SESSION_PREPARATION_GRACE_MS);

        E98 · app/client/pages/tickets/shared.ts:149–184

        应用自写 useTicketsApi 只用组件内 revision 重新请求；创建/分派成功后没有共享查询键或 invalidate，列表保持旧结果直到整页刷新。

        export function useTicketsApi<T>(path: string | null) {
  const api = useApiClient();
  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{
    path: string | null;
    revision: number;
    data?: T;
    error?: unknown;
    loading: boolean;
  }>({ path, revision: -1, loading: true });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!path) return;
    let active = true;
    void api.request<T>({ path }).then(
      (body) => {
        // Keep the response envelope: list endpoints carry `meta` beside
        // `data`, and every caller reads the ticket payload from `data.data`.
        if (active) setResult({ path, revision, data: body, loading: false });
      },
      (error: unknown) => {
        if (active) setResult({ path, revision, error, loading: false });
      },
    );
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [api, path, revision]);

  return {
    data: result.path === path ? result.data : undefined,
    error: result.path === path ? result.error : undefined,
    loading:
      path !== null && (result.path !== path || result.revision !== revision),
    reload: useCallback(() => setRevision((value) => value + 1), []),
  };

        业务场景与交付说明

        员工报修与附件

        员工可提交标题、描述、紧急程度并上传 sample.png，附件在工单详情和预览弹窗中可正常查看。

        服务台分派与通知

        服务台从首页进入工单并分派给工程师，工程师 Inbox 收到 Ticket assigned 通知，点击通知可直达对应工单。

        处理、退回与关闭

        工程师记录处理评论和 Resolution；员工通过通知查看结果后 Reopen 退回，工程师再次解决，员工最终 Close 关闭。

        角色隔离与首页概览

        员工仅看到自己的工单，工程师仅看到分派给自己的工单，越权直接访问被拒绝；服务台查看全部，首页展示待分派、超时和工程师工作量。

        01

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        缺陷：新建工单后立即返回 Tickets 列表（侧栏 Tickets 或工单详情的 Back to tickets），列表仍是旧数据。员工创建第 2、第 3 张工单后列表仍只显示之前的 1/2 张，必须整页刷新后才出现新工单；服务台完成分派后其 Tickets 列表也仍显示 Pending/Assignee —，整页刷新后才变为 Processing 和已分派。截图 c01-employee-stale-list-after-create.png 记录了刷新前列表缺少刚创建的 Cannot open shared mailbox calendar 工单。

        A1 · 9 条操作 · 7 条观察 · 18 张证据
        Agent 报告未通过
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        实际操作

        1. 管理员登录，在 Users 创建员工、Support engineer、Service desk 三个测试账号并确认角色写入
        2. 员工登录后提交报修工单：标题、描述、紧急程度 High、上传 fixtures/sample.png，提交成功并打开工单详情
        3. 员工打开附件预览确认 sample.png 可显示，并访问他人示例工单 /main/tickets/demo-ticket-vpn 验证被拒绝
        4. 服务台登录，首页查看待分派、超时、解决中数量和工程师工作量，打开新工单并分派给 Factory Engineer
        5. 工程师登录，通过 Inbox 的 Ticket assigned 通知进入同一工单，填写处理过程评论并提交 Resolution 完成解决
        6. 员工通过 Inbox 的 Ticket resolved 通知进入工单，执行 Reopen 并填写未解决原因，工单回到 Pending
        7. 服务台重新分派给同一工程师，工程师补充处理记录并再次解决；员工再次收到解决通知后进入工单，填写 Close note 并关闭
        8. 切换回员工，检查 Tickets 列表仅包含自己的 3 张工单；切换工程师，检查列表仅包含分派给自己的工单且直接访问他人工单被拒绝
        9. 检查控制台与页面错误：无 page error，仅有 realtime session preparation 超时 warning

        观察结果

        1. 员工提交后工单详情显示 TK-20261020-ABCBAA、High、Pending、描述和 sample.png 附件，附件预览弹窗可打开并显示图片
        2. 服务台首页显示 Open tickets 7、Unassigned 4、Overdue 2、Resolved 1，并列出 Bob Liu 2 active / Carol Wang 1 active / Factory Engineer 0 active，Needs attention 显示超过 24 小时和 Escalated 工单
        3. 服务台分派后工单详情变为 Processing，Lifecycle history 记录 Pending → Processing 和 Assign completed；工程师 Inbox 收到 Ticket assigned 通知，点击通知进入对应工单
        4. 工程师提交处理评论后，工单 Comments 显示诊断与处理过程；Resolve 弹窗填写 Resolution 后状态变为 Resolved，详情显示 Outcome/Resolution，员工 Inbox 收到 Ticket resolved 通知
        5. 员工执行 Reopen 后 Lifecycle history 记录 Resolved → Pending，Assignee 清空；第二次解决后员工执行 Close，Lifecycle history 记录 Resolved → Closed 并出现 Close completed
        6. 员工 Tickets 列表仅显示自己的工单（直接访问他人示例工单提示 Ticket demo-ticket-vpn was not found）；工程师列表仅显示分派给自己的 1 张工单，直接访问他人工单同样被拒绝
        7. 缺陷：新建工单后立即返回 Tickets 列表（侧栏 Tickets 或工单详情的 Back to tickets），列表仍是旧数据。员工创建第 2、第 3 张工单后列表仍只显示之前的 1/2 张，必须整页刷新后才出现新工单；服务台完成分派后其 Tickets 列表也仍显示 Pending/Assignee —，整页刷新后才变为 Processing 和已分派。截图 c01-employee-stale-list-after-create.png 记录了刷新前列表缺少刚创建的 Cannot open shared mailbox calendar 工单。

        来源：verify-6/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：failed
